Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the magnet space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 901 and the convergence rate is O(688/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the magnet space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 901 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 688. QED.
